<script>
        // on page refresh scroll page to top
        /*$(window).on('beforeunload', function () {
            $(window).scrollTop(0);
        });*/
        //change header on home page, category & vendors
        // $('.wsmenucontainer').addClass('scrollheader');

        $(window).scroll(function () {
            var scrollpage = $(window).scrollTop();
            if (scrollpage > 100) {
                //$(".header").removeClass("siteheader");
                $(".wsmenucontainer").addClass("scrollheader");
                $('.menuContainer').addClass('scrollnav');
                $('.smilelogo img').attr("src", "https://fastsmile.smile-pharmacy.gr/ui/front/img/SmilePharmacyLogo.png");
                $('.smilelogo img').css("width", "120px");
            }
            else {
                //$(".header").addClass("siteheader");
                $(".wsmenucontainer").removeClass("scrollheader");
                $('.menuContainer').removeClass('scrollnav');
                $('.smilelogo img').attr("src", "https://fastsmile.smile-pharmacy.gr/ui/front/img/SmilePharmacyLogo.png");
                $('.smilelogo img').css("width", "inherit");
            }
        });
        $(document).ready(function () {
            $('.wsmenucontainer').removeClass('scrollheader');
            $('.menuContainer').removeClass('scrollnav');
            $('.smilelogo img').attr("src", "https://fastsmile.smile-pharmacy.gr/ui/front/img/SmilePharmacyLogo.png");
            $('.smilelogo img').css("width", "inherit");
        });
    </script>
<script>
    // https://davidwalsh.name/javascript-debounce-function
    // Returns a function, that, as long as it continues to be invoked, will not
    // be triggered. The function will be called after it stops being called for
    // N milliseconds. If `immediate` is passed, trigger the function on the
    // leading edge, instead of the trailing.
    function debounce(func, wait, immediate) {
        var timeout;
        return function () {
            var context = this, args = arguments;
            var later = function () {
                timeout = null;
                if (!immediate) func.apply(context, args);
            };
            var callNow = immediate && !timeout;
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
            if (callNow) func.apply(context, args);
        };
    }

    $('.smile-ordertabblock').on("click", '.orders_list_more', function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        var parent_id = "#" + $(this).data('parent-id');
        var this_element = $(this);
        $.ajax({
            method: "POST",
            url: "https://www.smile-pharmacy.gr/customer/getCustomerOrdersMore.htm",
            data: {order_type: $(this).data('order_type'), current_page: $(this).data('current_page')}
        })
            .done(function (msg) {
                this_element.remove();
                $(parent_id).append(msg);
            });
    });

    // product quickview

            beforeOpen: function () {
                this.st.mainClass = this.st.el.attr('data-effect');
            }
        },
        // closeOnContentClick: true,
        midClick: true // allow opening popup on middle mouse click. Always set it to true if you don't provide alternative source.
    });

    function scrollToElement(aid) {
        var aTag = $(aid);
        $('html,body').animate({scrollTop: aTag.offset().top - 150}, 'slow');
    }

    function validateEmail(email) {
        var re = /^(([^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+(\.[^<>()\[\]\\.,;:\s@"]+)*)|(".+"))@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}])|(([a-zA-Z\-0-9]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}))$/;
        return re.test(email);
    }

    function alertNotify(elem, data) {
        var style = 'green';
        var msg = ("msg" in data) ? data.msg : '';

        if ("status" in data) {
            switch (data.status) {
                case 'warning':
                    style = 'warning';
                    break;
                case 'danger':
                    style = 'danger';
                    break;
                case 'success':
                default:
                    style = 'green';
            }
        }

        if (typeof(elem) == 'object') {
            (elem).notify(msg, {
                autoHide: true,
                autoHideDelay: 5000,
                //globalPosition: 'top right',
                elementPosition: 'top center',
                gap: 2,
                style: style
            });
        } else {
            $.notify(msg, {
                autoHide: true,
                autoHideDelay: 5000,
                globalPosition: 'bottom right',
                style: style
            });
        }
    }

    function cartNotify(elem) {
        if (typeof(elem) == 'object') {
            $(elem).notify('Το προϊόν μπήκε<br>στο καλάθι σας <i class="fa fa-shopping-cart"></i>', {
                autoHide: true,
                autoHideDelay: 4000,
                //globalPosition: 'top right',
                elementPosition: 'top center',
                gap: 2,
                style: 'pink'
            });
        } else {
            $.notify('Το προϊόν μπήκε στο καλάθι σας', {
                autoHide: true,
                autoHideDelay: 4000,
                globalPosition: 'bottom right',
                //elementPosition: 'top right',
                style: 'pink'
            });
        }
    }

    // Minicart functions -->
    function update_minicart() {
        $('.js-cart-menu-link').empty().append(minicartLoad);
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: minicartUrl,
            dataType: 'html',
            success: function (data) {
                $('.js-cart-menu-link').empty().append(data);

                setMinicartOuterValues();

                $("a#closeminicart").on('click', function () {
                    $('.minicartblock').removeClass('showminicartblock');
                    return false;
                });
            }
        });
    }

    function setMinicartOuterValues() {
        if (typeof $("#minicart-actual-total").html() !== 'undefined') {
            $(".js-general-cart-total-price").html($("#minicart-actual-total").html());
        } else {
            $(".js-general-cart-total-price").html("0.00");
        }

        if (typeof $("#minicart-total-qty").html() !== 'undefined') {
            $("#card-outer-quantitytext").removeClass("hide").html($("#minicart-total-qty").html());
        } else {
            $("#card-outer-quantitytext").removeClass("hide").addClass("hide");
        }
    }

    function removeItemFromMiniCart(cartId) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: minicartDeleteLink,
            dataType: "json",
            data: {"rowid": cartId},
            complete: function () {
                                update_minicart();
                            }
        });
        return false;
    }

    var oldIds = {"1":"7234","169":"6033","222":"6671","412":"5109","495":"5189","540":"444","1298":"1099","1416":"1198","1638":"7034","1709":"1456","2436":"556","3349":"780","3681":"3104","3912":"3324","4959":"4238","5558":"5087","5929":"4155","6128":"6525","6218":"5873","6245":"2491","6308":"5108","7100":"8093","7227":"8250","7355":"8378","9105":"9105","10258":"10258","10881":"10881","12276":"12276","12530":"12530","12808":"12808","13977":"13977","14164":"14164","14508":"14508","14731":"14731","17078":"17078","17184":"17184","17925":"17925","17936":"17936","18812":"18812","20104":"20104","20710":"20710","20736":"20736","20891":"20891","21006":"21006","21061":"21061","21109":"21109","21153":"21153","21172":"21172","21174":"21174","21194":"21194","21217":"21217","21218":"21218","21284":"21284","21328":"21328","21333":"21333"};

    function addToCartDiscounted(pid, price, elem) {
        var uri = ''
        addToCartAjax(setUpCartFormData(pid, price, uri), elem)
        $('#jsPagediscountCloseText').html('')
        $('#discPopClose').addClass('green')
        ajaxProductAddCount++
    }

    function setUpCartFormData(product_id, product_price, uri) {
        var product_quantity = 1;

        if ($("[name='qty_input']").length) {
            product_quantity = $("[name='qty_input']").val();
        }

        if ($("[name='quant[1]']").length) {
            product_quantity = $("[name='quant[1]']").val(); // @TODO  NFGFG
        }

        var pr_attrs = selectedAttributes();
        return {
            "product_id": product_id,
            "product_price": product_price,
            'qty_input': product_quantity,
            'attrs': pr_attrs,
            'uri': uri
        };
    }

    function addToCartAjax(formData, elem) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: cartUrlSingle,
            data: formData,
            success: function () {
                update_minicart();
                
                                    if (oldIds.hasOwnProperty(formData.product_id)) {
                        fbq('track', 'AddToCart', {
                            content_type: 'product',
                            content_ids: ['DB_'+oldIds[formData.product_id]],
                            value: formData.product_price,
                            currency: 'EUR'
                        });
                    }
                
                if (is_cart == true) {
                    window.location.reload(true);
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // This function add an element to cart
    function addToCart(pid, price, elem) {
        addToCartAjax(setUpCartFormData(pid, price, ''), elem)
    }

    // live search function -->
    var delay = (function () {
        var timer = 0;
        return function (callback, ms) {
            clearTimeout(timer);
            timer = setTimeout(callback, ms);
        };
    })();

    function getSearchTerm() {
        return $("#searchtxt").val();
    }

    function buildSearchUrl() {
        return 'https://www.smile-pharmacy.gr/search/' + stripNonUtf8Letters(getSearchTerm()) + '.htm';
    }

    function sendToSearchPage() {
        $(location).attr('href', encodeURI(buildSearchUrl()));
    }

    function searchButtonClick() {
        sendToSearchPage(getSearchTerm());
    }

    function searchTextKeyUpHook(e) {
        var code = e.keyCode || e.which;
        if (code == 13) {
            sendToSearchPage(getSearchTerm());
        } else {
            searchLookup();
        }
    }

    var searchLookup = debounce(function () {
        productLookup();
    }, 1000);

    function productLookup() {

        searchTerm = getSearchTerm();

        if (getSearchTerm().length > 2) {
            //$('#search_button').html($('#loader')).html();
            $('#search_button img').attr('src', '/ui/front/img/bx_loader.gif');
            //this is blocking execution
            $.post(buildSearchUrl(), {queryString: "" + searchTerm + ""}, function (data) {
                $('#search_button img').attr('src', '/ui/front/img/search_icon.png');
                /*if (data === '') {
                    $('#searchcontainermain').hide().html("");
                } else {*/
                $('#searchcontainermain').html(data).show();

                if(data.indexOf('alert alert-warning')==-1) {
                    $("#innersearchresultsblock").mCustomScrollbar({
                        setHeight: 350,
                        theme: "dark"
                    });
                }
                /*}*/
            });
        } else {
            $('#search_button img').attr('src', '/ui/front/img/search_icon.png');
            $('#searchcontainermain').hide().html("");
        }
    }

    });

        if(e.target.className !== "searchcontainermain"){
            $(this).removeClass("opensearch");
        }
    });

    $(document).ready(function () {
        //we always have these elements in header so no need to check for existance
        document.getElementById('searchtxt').addEventListener('keyup', function(e){searchTextKeyUpHook(e)});

    $('.btn-number').click(function (e) {
        //e.preventDefault();
        fieldName = $(this).attr('data-field');
        type = $(this).attr('data-type');
        var input = $(this).parent().parent().find("input");
        var currentVal = parseInt(input.val());
        if (!isNaN(currentVal)) {
            if (type == 'minus') {

                if (currentVal > input.attr('min')) {
                    input.val(currentVal - 1);

//                    if (currentVal < input.attr('max')) {
//                        $(this).parent().parent().find('button.[data-type="plus"]').attr('disabled', false);
//                    }
                }

//                if (currentVal == input.attr('min')) {
//                    $(this).attr('disabled', true);
//                }

            } else if (type == 'plus') {

                if (currentVal < input.attr('max')) {
                    input.val(currentVal + 1);

//                    if (currentVal > input.attr('min')) {
//                        $(this).parent().parent().find('button.[data-type="minus"]').attr('disabled', false);
//                    }
                }

//                if (currentVal == input.attr('max')) {
//                    $(this).attr('disabled', true);
//                }

            }
        } else {
            input.val(0);
        }
    });

    $('.input-number').focusin(function () {
        $(this).data('oldValue', $(this).val());
    });

    $('.input-number').change(function () {
        minValue = parseInt($(this).attr('min'));
        maxValue = parseInt($(this).attr('max'));
        valueCurrent = parseInt($(this).val());

        // name = $(this).attr('name');
        if (valueCurrent = minValue) {
            //$(".btn-number[data-type='minus'][data-field='" + name + "']").removeAttr('disabled')
        } else {
            $(this).val($(this).data('oldValue'));
        }
        if (valueCurrent = maxValue) {
            //$(".btn-number[data-type='plus'][data-field='" + name + "']").removeAttr('disabled')
        } else {
            $(this).val($(this).data('oldValue'));
        }
    });

    $(".input-number").keydown(function (e) {
        // Allow: backspace, delete, tab, escape, enter and .
        if ($.inArray(e.keyCode, [46, 8, 9, 27, 13, 190]) !== -1 ||
            // Allow: Ctrl+A
            (e.keyCode == 65 && e.ctrlKey === true) ||
            // Allow: home, end, left, right
            (e.keyCode >= 35 && e.keyCode <= 39)) {
            // let it happen, don't do anything
            return;
        }
        // Ensure that it is a number and stop the keypress
        if ((e.shiftKey || (e.keyCode < 48 || e.keyCode > 57)) && (e.keyCode < 96 || e.keyCode > 105)) {
            e.preventDefault();
        }
    });

    //select style
    $('.selectpicker').selectpicker({
        //  style: 'btn-info',
        size: 3
    });

    //product page magnify
    $('.productslider .slider-for img').magnify();
    /*product page add more products scroll*/

                theme: "dark"
            });
        });
    })(jQuery);

    function addToWaitingList(email, product, token) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: 'https://www.smile-pharmacy.gr/waiting_list/add.htm',
            data: {"product": product, "email": email, "g-recaptcha-response": token},
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function (data) {

                if(data.status=='success') {
                    $(".waitinglistblock").hide();
                    $("#waiting_list_res").html('<div class="alert alert-success" role="alert">'+data.msg+'</div>');
                }
else{
                    $("#waiting_list_res").html('<div class="alert alert-warning" role="alert">'+data.msg+'</div>');
                }

            }
        });
    }

    // This method updates the Availability messages
    function updateAvailability(pid) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: 'https://www.smile-pharmacy.gr/order/cart_update_availability.htm',
            dataType: 'html',
            data: {'product_id': pid},
            success: function (data) {

                $('.productavailabilityrow').empty().append(data);
                if ($(".unavailable").length) {
                    $(".addtocart")
                        .prop('disabled', true)
                        .addClass('disabled')
                        .html('ΜΗ ΔΙΑΘΕΣΙΜΟ');

                                    $('.waitinglistblock').show();
                                    }
            }
        });
    }

    // This function updates the quantity picker
    function updateQuantityPicker(pid) {
        var obj = $('[name="qty_input"]');
        var json = {};
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: 'https://www.smile-pharmacy.gr/order/cart_fetch_quantity_picker.htm',
            dataType: 'html',
            data: {'product_id': pid},
            success: function (data) {
                json = JSON.parse(data);
                obj.attr({"max": json.stock});
            }
        });
    }

    // This function gets the selected attributes
    function selectedAttributes() {
        var attrs_array = [];
        $('.attrsload').each(function () {
            if ($(this)) {
                var elemName = $(this).attr('name');
                var splitElemName = elemName.split("_");
                var attrs_id = String(splitElemName[1]);
                var attr_id = $(this).val();
                if (attr_id > 0) {
                    attrs_array[attrs_id] = attr_id;
                }
            }
        });
        if (attrs_array.length > 0) {
            return attrs_array;
        } else {
            return [];
        }
    }
</script>

        function brandSearch(obj, target) {
            $(target).children().hide();
            $(target).children().filter(function (index) {
                return ($(this).text().toUpperCase().indexOf($(obj).val().toUpperCase()) > 0);
            }).show();
        }

    function createWarning(message) {
        var html = '<div class="modal fade" tabindex="-1" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="alert-modal" id="alertModal">' +
            '<div class="modal-dialog modal-lg" role="document">' +
            '<div class="modal-content alert alert-info" style="margin-top:50%;padding:8px;background-color:#d9edf7">' +
            '<div class="modal-header" style="padding:0;border-bottom:0;">' +
            '<button type="button" class="close" data-dismiss="modal" aria-label="Close">' +
            '<span aria-hidden="true">&times;</span>' +
            '</button>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="modal-body text-center" style="padding:0  0 18px 0;">' +
            '<strong>' + message + '</strong>' +
            '</div> </div> </div> </div>';

        $("body").append(html);
        $("#alertModal").modal('show').on('hidden.bs.modal', function (e) {
            $(this).remove();
        });
    }

<script type="text/javascript">

    $(".quickview").on('click', function () {

        var quickviewElement = this;

        var oldPrice = $(quickviewElement).data('old-price').toString();
        var oldPriceArr = oldPrice.split('.');
        var newPrice = $(quickviewElement).data('new-price').toString();
        var newPriceArr = newPrice.split('.');
        var catSlug = $(quickviewElement).data('cat-slug');
        var prodSlug = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-slug');

        var url = 'https://www.smile-pharmacy.gr/' + catSlug + '/' + prodSlug + '.htm';
        var prodId = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-id');
        var prodTitle = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-title');
        var prodSubscription = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-subscription');
        var prodImage = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-image');

        var prodAvailability = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-availability');
        var prodStock = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-stock');
        var prodNegativeStock = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-negativestock');

        var prodPoints = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-points');
        var prodPointFactor = $(quickviewElement).data('prod-point-factor');

        $('#smiliePointMsg').css("display", "none");
        $('#smiliesPointsMsg').css("display", "none");

        if (prodPointFactor >= 1 && prodAvailability > 0) {
            $('#prSmPNo').html("" + prodPoints);
            $('#smiliePointMsg').css("display", "block");
            $('#smiliesPointsMsg').css("display", "none");
        }

        /*if(prodPointFactor==1){
            $('#prSmPNo').html(prodPoints);
            $('#smiliePointMsg').css("display", "block");
            $('#smiliesPointsMsg').css("display", "none");
        }
        if(prodPointFactor>1){
            $('#smiliesPoints').html(prodPoints);
            $('#smiliePointMsg').css("display", "none");
            $('#smiliesPointsMsg').css("display", "block");
        }*/


        var oldPriceInt = oldPriceArr[0];
        var oldPriceDec = oldPriceArr[1];
        var newPriceInt = newPriceArr[0];
        var newPriceDec = newPriceArr[1];

        $('#quickView-title').html(prodTitle);
        $('#quickView-image').attr("src", prodImage);
        $('#quickView-subscription').html(prodSubscription);
        $('#quickView-old-price-int').html(oldPriceInt);
        $('#quickView-old-price-dec').html('.' + oldPriceDec);
        $('#quickView-new-price-int').html(newPriceInt);
        $('#quickView-new-price-dec').html('.' + newPriceDec);
        $('#quickView-availability').attr("max", prodAvailability);
        $('#quickView-prod-id').html(prodId);

        var availabilityWrapperObj = $('#quickView-availability-wrapper');
        var availableObj = $('#quickView-available');
        var unavailableObj = $('#quickView-unavailable');
        var addToCartObj = $('#quickView-add-to-cart');
        var unavailableButton = $('#quickView-unavailable-button');

        var popUpObj = $('#productquickview');

        $(availabilityWrapperObj).css("display", "block");
        $(availableObj).css("display", "block");
        if (prodStock > 0) {
            $(availableObj).html('ΑΜΕΣΑ ΔΙΑΘΕΣΙΜΟ');
        } else if (prodAvailability > 0) {
            $(availableObj).html('ΔΙΑΘΕΣΙΜΟ');
        } else {
            $(availableObj).html('ΜΗ ΔΙΑΘΕΣΙΜΟ');
        }


        $(unavailableObj).css("display", "none");
        $(unavailableButton).css("display", "none");

        $(addToCartObj).on('click', function () {
            addToCart(prodId, newPrice, this);
            //$(popUpObj).magnificPopup('close');
        });

        if (prodAvailability <= 0) {
            $(availabilityWrapperObj).css("display", "none");
            $(availableObj).css("display", "none");
            $(unavailableObj).css("display", "block");
            $(unavailableButton).css("display", "block");
            $(addToCartObj).css("display", "none");
        }

        if (prodAvailability > 0) {
            $(availabilityWrapperObj).css("display", "block");
            $(availableObj).css("display", "block");

            $(unavailableObj).css("display", "none");
            $(unavailableButton).css("display", "none");
            $(addToCartObj).css("display", "block");
        }

        $(popUpObj).magnificPopup({
            items: {src: '#productquickview', type: 'inline'},
            mainClass: 'mfp-zoom-out',
            removalDelay: 500
        }).magnificPopup('open');

        $('.mfp-close').on('click', function () {
            $(popUpObj).magnificPopup('close');
        });

    });

</script>

    $("[id^=toggletab]").on('click', function () {

        return false;

<script>
    function goToVendorLine() {
        var url = $('#vendorLineSelector').find(':selected').data('line-url');
        window.location = url;
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        if ($("#js-vendor-products-search-txt").length) {
            document.getElementById('js-vendor-products-search-txt').addEventListener('keyup', vendorProductsLookUp);
        }
    });

    var vendorProductsLookUp = debounce(function () {
        var inputString = $("#js-vendor-products-search-txt").val();
        var vendorId = $("#searchVendorId").val();
        if (inputString.length > 2) {
            var vendorSearchUrl = 'https://www.smile-pharmacy.gr/search/liveSearchVendorProducts.htm';
            $.post(vendorSearchUrl, {queryString: "" + inputString + "", vendorId: vendorId}, function (data) {
                if (data === '') {
                    $('#vendorsearchresults').hide();
                } else {
                    $('#vendorsearchresults').empty().html(data).show();
                }
                //$('#searchvendorseries > i.fa').removeClass('fa-spinner').removeClass('fa-spin').addClass('fa-search');
            });
        }
    }, 1000);


    $(document).mouseup(function (e) {
        var container = $('#vendorsearchresults');
        if (!container.is(e.target) && container.has(e.target).length === 0) {
            container.hide();
        }
    });

    if ($(window).width() > 1023) {
        (function ($) {
            $(window).on("load", function () {
                $(".smilesideblock").mCustomScrollbar({
                    setHeight: 305,
                    theme: "dark"
                });
            });
        })(jQuery);
    };

        $(this).find('.popupAddtoCart img').addClass("animated tadaCart");
        $('.popupAddtoCart').fadeIn();

<script>
    //bio page accordion
    //uses classList, setAttribute, and querySelectorAll
//if you want this to work in IE8/9 youll need to polyfill these
(function(){
    var d = document,
    accordionToggles = d.querySelectorAll('.js-accordionTrigger'),
    setAria,
    setAccordionAria,
    switchAccordion,
  touchSupported = ('ontouchstart' in window),
  pointerSupported = ('pointerdown' in window);

  skipClickDelay = function(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    e.target.click();
  }

        setAriaAttr = function(el, ariaType, newProperty){
        el.setAttribute(ariaType, newProperty);
    };
    setAccordionAria = function(el1, el2, expanded){
        switch(expanded) {
      case "true":
        setAriaAttr(el1, 'aria-expanded', 'true');
        setAriaAttr(el2, 'aria-hidden', 'false');
        break;
      case "false":
        setAriaAttr(el1, 'aria-expanded', 'false');
        setAriaAttr(el2, 'aria-hidden', 'true');
        break;
      default:
                break;
        }
    };
//function
switchAccordion = function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var thisAnswer = e.target.parentNode.nextElementSibling;
    var thisQuestion = e.target;
    if(thisAnswer.classList.contains('is-collapsed')) {
        setAccordionAria(thisQuestion, thisAnswer, 'true');
    } else {
        setAccordionAria(thisQuestion, thisAnswer, 'false');
    }
    thisQuestion.classList.toggle('is-collapsed');
    thisQuestion.classList.toggle('is-expanded');
        thisAnswer.classList.toggle('is-collapsed');
        thisAnswer.classList.toggle('is-expanded');

    thisAnswer.classList.toggle('animateIn');
    };
    for (var i=0,len=accordionToggles.length; i<len; i++) {
        if(touchSupported) {
      accordionToggles[i].addEventListener('touchstart', skipClickDelay, false);
    }
    if(pointerSupported){
      accordionToggles[i].addEventListener('pointerdown', skipClickDelay, false);
    }
    accordionToggles[i].addEventListener('click', switchAccordion, false);
  }
})();
</script>
<!--offers countdown scripts-->
<script>

    var sessionCookies = {"1":false,"2":false};

    $("#cookiedismiss").on("click", function() {
        sessionCookies[1] = 1;
        sessionCookies[2] = 1;
        postCookieOptions();
        return false;
    });

    $("#popupCookieSubmitBtn").on("click", function () {
        sessionCookies[1] = 1;
        sessionCookies[2] = $("#cookieAdvertising").is(":checked") ? 1 : 0;
        postCookieOptions();
        return false;
    });

    //cookie notice pop up
    $(document).ready(function() {
        $("#showCookieSettings").click(function () {
            $('#cookiesettings-dialog').addClass('visibleCookiesSettings');
            return false;
        });
    });

    function postCookieOptions () {
        var request = $.ajax({
            url: "https://www.smile-pharmacy.gr/customer/set_cookies.htm",
            method: "POST",
            data: sessionCookies,
            dataType: "json"
        });

        request.done(function( msg ) {
            sessionCookies[1] = msg[1];
            sessionCookies[2] = msg[2];
            $('#cookiesettings-dialog').removeClass('visibleCookiesSettings');
            $("#cookie-notice").fadeOut("normal", function () {});

        });

        request.fail(function( jqXHR, textStatus ) {
            sessionCookies[1] = false;
            sessionCookies[2] = false;
            // @TODO show message to user to try again
            $('#cookiesettings-dialog').removeClass('visibleCookiesSettings');
        });
    }

</script>

//promo points img popup
$(document).ready(function() {
    $('.image-popup-vertical-fit').magnificPopup({
        type: 'image',
        closeOnContentClick: true,
        mainClass: 'mfp-img-mobile',

//preview order smile points login & create account banner scripts
$("#smPtsLogin").click(function () {
    $('#registerChecktab input[type="radio"]').attr('checked', true);
    $('.prv_ord_password').css("display", "block");
    $('.prv_ord_password_retype').css("display", "block");
    $('.preview-login-box').css('display', 'none');
    $(".customer-info-row").css('display', 'block');
    $(".login_existing").val('0');
    $(".register_and_continue").val('1');
    $('#registerChecktab').addClass('selectedordertab');
    $('#guestchecktab').removeClass('selectedordertab');
    $('#loginChecktab').removeClass('selectedordertab');
});

$("a#smPtsPopLog").click(function () {
    $('.preview-user-type input[type="radio"]').attr('checked', false);
    $('#loginChecktab input[type="radio"]').attr('checked', true);
    $('.prv_ord_password').css("display", "none");
    $('.prv_ord_password_retype').css("display", "none");
    $(".customer-info-row").css('display', 'none');
    $(".login_existing").val('1');
    $(".register_and_continue").val('0');
    $('#loginChecktab').addClass('selectedordertab');
    $('#guestchecktab').removeClass('selectedordertab');
    $('#registerChecktab').removeClass('selectedordertab');
});

$('a#smPtsPopLog').on('click', function () {
    $('.wrap').toggleClass('active');
    $(this).addClass('active');
    return false;
});

// enable cart items script
function enableCartLoad() {
    setTimeout(function()  {
        $('#cartItemsPopOver').fadeIn(300).addClass('openCartPop');
    }, 500);
}

function closeCartPopUp() {
    $('#cartItemsPopOver').fadeOut(300).removeClass('openCartPop');
}

// on click esc close popup
$(document).keydown(function(e) {
    if (e.keyCode == 27) {
        $('#cartItemsPopOver').fadeOut(300).removeClass('openCartPop');
    }
});

$('#cartItemsPopOver').on('click', function(e) {
  if (e.target !== this)
    return;
    $(this).fadeOut(300).removeClass('openCartPop');
});
</script>
